1b1(rgyud): @##//bod skad du/byang chub kyi snying po'i rgyan 'bum gyi gzungs/sangs rgyas dang 
1b2(rgyud): byang chub sems dpa' thams cad la phyag 'tshal lo//oM na mo b+ha ga bA te/bi pu la ba da na 
1b3(rgyud): kAM tsa nod k+Shing ta/pra b+hA sa ke tu mu ta ra ya/ta thA ga tA ya/a ra ha te/samya ka saM bud d+ha ya/na mo 
1b4(rgyud): b+ha ga bA te shAkya mu ni yai/ta thA ga tA ya/ar ha te/samya ka saM bu da d+ha ya/tad+ya thA/bo d+hi bo d+hi/bo d+hi 
1b5(rgyud): ni bo d+hi ni/sarba ta thA ga tA go tsa re/d+ha ra d+ha ra/ha ra ha ra/pra ha ra pra ha ra/ma hA bo d+hi tsi ta d+ha re/tsu lu 
1b6(rgyud): tsu lu/Sha Ta ra smi saM tso di te/sarba ta thA ga tA b+hi shig te/gu Ne gu Na ba te/sarba bud d+ha NA ba b+hA se/mi 
1b7(rgyud): li mi li/ga ga na ta le/pra tiSh+Tha tai/sarba ta thA ga tA a d+hiSh+Tha te/na b+ha sta le/sha ma sha ma/pra sha ma 
1b8(rgyud): pra sha me/sarba pA paM pra sha ma ne/sarba pA paM bi sho d+ha ne/hu lu hu lu/ma hA bo d+hi marga saM pra tiSh+Tha tai//
2a1(rgyud): @#//sarba ta thA ga tA su pra tiSh+Thi ti shud d+he swA hA/mu kha man tra/oM sarba ta thA ga ta byA ba lo ki te/dza ya dza ya swAhA/hri da ya~M/oM hu lu hu ludza ya mu khe swAhA/u pa hri da ya~M/oM badzra a yu She 
2a2(rgyud): swA hA/dge slong ngam/dge slong ma 'am/dge bsnyen nam/dge bsnyen ma 'am/gzhan yang rigs kyi bu 'am rigs kyi bu mo gang la la zhig gis gzungs 'di bris te/mchod rten byas la mchod rten 
2a3(rgyud): de'i nang du gzungs 'di bcug na/des mchod rten gcig byas na 'bum byas par 'gyur ro//lha rdzas kyi me tog dang/dri dang/phreng ba dang/byug pa dang/phye ma dang/na bza' dang/gdugs dang/rgyal mtshan 
2a4(rgyud): dang ba dan la sogs pas kyang mchod par 'gyur te/mchod rten 'ba' zhig mchod par ma yin gyi/sangs rgyas dkon mchog dang/chos dkon mchog dang/dge 'dun dkon mchog rnams la yang mchod 
2a5(rgyud): pa'i yo byad de dag gis mchod par 'gyur ro//dga' bo ngas mdo 'di bshad pa ni bsod nams zhan pa dang/ma dang pha dang/lta ba log pa dang/the tsom za ba dang/mtshams med pa byed pa dang//tshe thung ba dang /
2a6(rgyud): sems can dmyal ba dang/byol song dang/gshin rje'i 'jig rten dang/mtshams med par 'gro ba'i sems can de lta bu dag gi phyir gzungs 'di bshad do//dga' bo phyi ma'i dus phyi ma'i tshe khyim na gnas 
2a7(rgyud): pa dang/dge slong las phal cher gzungs 'di mi klog pa dang/mchod par mi byed pa rnams ji ltar khyim pa rnams sdug bsngal bar bshad pa de bzhin du/rab tu byung ba rnams kyang sdug bsngal bar 
2a8(rgyud): 'gyur ro//de ste de bzhin du sangs rgyas mchod cing 'di las nyan pas mchod rten gcig byas na chos kyis brgyan pa brgyad khri bzhi stong rnams kyang mchod par 'gyur ro//gzhan yang bsod nams kyi phung po 
2b1(rgyud): dpag tu med par 'gyur ro//sdong po brgyan pa bklags shing kha ton du byas par 'gyur ro//'jig rten dang /'jig rten las 'das pa'i dkyil 'khor du yang zhugs par 'gyur ro////
2b2(rgyud): byang chub kyi snying po'i rgyan 'bum las btus pa 'bum gyi gzungs rdzogs s+ho////@#//bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas 
2b3(rgyud): rnam par snang mdzad 'od kyi rgyal po la phyag 'tshal lo//de la mchod rten gcig btab na bye ba btab par 'gyur ba'i sngags dang /cho ga ni 'jim pa la sogs pa yo byad thams cad la sngags 'di lan nyi shu rtsa 
2b4(rgyud): gcig bzlas par bya'o//oM na mo b+ha ga ba te bai ro tsa na pra b+ha rA dzA ya/ta thA ga tA ya/a ra ha te samyaka sa~M bud d+ha ya/tad+ya thA/oM suk+Sha me sUk+Sha me sa me sa ma ye/shAn te dA na te a sa mA ro be a nA lo be/ta ra 
2b5(rgyud): la~M be/ya sho ba ti/ma hA te dzo na rA ku le ni ra bA Ne/sarba bud d+ha a d+hiSh+Tha na a d+hiSh+Thi te swA hA/tse t+ya 'debs pa'i 'jim pa'am/sngags bri ba'i tshe lan bdun du gdab pa'i sngags la/na maHsa man ta bud d+ha nA~M/oM badzre 
2b6(rgyud): a yu She swA hA/'bi 'bi'i sngags la/oM badzrod b+ha ba ye swA hA/tse t+ya phor bu'i nad dang /sa tsha'i nang du phye ma gdab pa'am/'bru mar byugs pa'i sngags la/oM a ra dze bi ra dze swA hA/phyag rgya'am/sngags 
2b7(rgyud): gzhug pa'i sngags la/oM d+harma d+hA tu gar b+he swA hA/tse t+ya pho ra bur 'bi 'bi gzhug pa'i sngags la/o~M badzra d+hA tu gar b+he swA hwA/tho bas rdeg cing gdab pa'i sngags la/oM badzra mud ka ra a ko Ta ya hU~M phaT 
2b8(rgyud): swA hA/tse t+ya phur bu nas phyir dbyung ba'i sngags la/oM d+ha rma ra te swA hA/'khor lo dang /dbu zhu gsol ba'i sngags la/oM b+h+ra ma na ye swA hA/tsai t+ya'i dkyil 'khor la bzhugs pa'i sngags la/
3a1(rgyud): @#//oM su pra tiSh+Tha ta badzre ya swA hA/cho ga de ltar byas na tsi t+ya gcig btab pas bye ba btab par 'gyur ro//thams cad tu skye ba dran par 'gyur ro//dkyil 'khor byas pa'i lag pa 'jim pa can gyis gdan bar bskus na nad

3a3(rgyud): h+ya d+hA tu ka ra Na Da nA ma d+hA ra ni ma hA yA na sU tra/bod skad du/'phags pa de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi byin gyis rlabs kyi snying po gsang ba ring bsrel gyi za ma tog ces bya ba'i gzungs theg pa chen po'i mdo/sangs rgyas 
3a4(rgyud): dang byang chub sems dpa' thams cad la phyag 'tshal lo//'di skad bdag gis thos pa'i dus gcig na/bcom ldan 'das yul ma ga d+ha na dri ma med pa'i kun dga' ra ba na rin po che sna bdun gyi rdzing na byang chub sems dpa'i 
3a5(rgyud): dge 'dun chen po dang /nyan thos kyi dge 'dun chen po dang /lha dang klu dang /gnod sbyin dang /dri za dang /lha ma yin dang /nam mkha' lding dang /mi'am ci dang /lto 'phye chen po dang /mi dang mi ma yin pa bye ba mang po rnams dang /yul gyi 
3a6(rgyud): mi 'bum phrag du ma rnams kyis yongs su bskor cing mdun du byas nas thabs gcig tu bzhugs so//de nas 'khor gyi nang de na bram ze chen po shing sA la chen po lta bu mkhas pa/mdzangs pa/gsal ba/yid gzhungs pa/
3a7(rgyud): mthong na dga' ba/dge ba bcu'i las kyi lam yongs su gzung ba/dri med legs snang zhes bya ba zhig yod de/de yang gcig tu dkon mchog gsum la dad cing gus la phyag byed pa'i dge ba'i bsam pa dang ldan pa/zhib mo brtag 
3a8(rgyud): pa sems can thams cad dge ba'i don la brtson pa/nor che ba/longs spyod che ba/phyug pa/rdzas mang ba/yo byad phun sum tshogs pa yin no//de nas bram ze chen po dri med legs snang /bcom ldan 'das 
3b1(rgyud): ga la ba der song ste phyin nas yang bcom ldan 'das la lan bdun bskor ba byas nas/bcom ldan 'das la me tog dang /bdug pas mngon par mchod do//na bza' rin 'bum ri ba yang gsol/bcom ldan 
3b2(rgyud): 'das la mu tig gi do shal 'bum ri ba yang gsol nas zhabs gnyis la mgo bos phyag 'tshal te/bcom ldan 'das kyi spyan sngar 'dug nas/bcom ldan 'das la'adi skad ces gsol to//bcom ldan 
3b3(rgyud): 'das sras tshogs kyi 'khor dang bcas pa/bdag gis sdum par gdugs tshod la spyan 'dren na de'i slad du bcom ldan 'das kyis gnang bar mdzad par ci gnang /de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis bram ze chen po dri med 
3b4(rgyud): lags snang gis spyan drangs par thugs su chud nas cang mi gsung bar gyur pas gnang ngo //de nas bram ze chen po dri med legs snang bcom ldan 'das kyis spyan snga nas cang mi gsung bas gnang bar rig nas 
3b5(rgyud): de rings pa rings par rang gi khyim du song ste/de nyid kyi mtshan mo 'das pa dang /zhal zas dang /yo byad dang /zhal zas ro brgya pa mang po mang po bshams nas bkod pa chen pos bkra shis kyi gzhal med khang dang /
3b6(rgyud): gzhal med khang chen po sna tshogs dang /me tog dang /bdug pa khyer te/'khor mang po dang /sil snyan dang /pheg rdob pas bcom ldan 'das ga la ba der song ste phyin nas/bcom ldan 'das la dus la bab 
3b7(rgyud): pa gsol to//de nas bcom ldan 'das la bram ze chen po dri med lags snang gis 'di skad ces gsol to//bcom ldan 'das 'di ni gshegs pa'i dus lags te/bcom ldan 'das gshegs pa'i dus

4a1(rgyud): pa thams cad deng don chen po mdzad par 'gyur gyis tshur sheg/de nas bcom ldan 'das gdan de las bzhengs nas/bcom ldan 'das gdan de nas bzhengs ma thag tu bcom ldan 'das kyi sku 
4a2(rgyud): las 'od zer tha dad pa sna tshogs byung ste/'od zer gyi snang ba des phyogs bcu'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing thams cad snang bar byas nas de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad yang dag par bskul te gzigs nas gshegs par 
4a3(rgyud): shes so//de nas bram ze chen po dri med legs snang gis mchod pa dang /bskur sti chen pos mchod cing bcom ldan 'das kyi gshegs shul mdzes pa cher byas so//'khor mang po dang /lha dang /klu dang /gnod sbyin

4a6(rgyud): yod de zhig pa/tsher mas gang ba/rtswa dang /shing dang /gyo mos kun tu gang ba phyag dar gyi phung po lta bu zhig yod do//de nas bcom ldan 'da' de'i drung du gshegs pa dang /mchod rten rnying pa phyag dar gyi phung po lta ba de yang 
4a7(rgyud): kun nas 'bar zhing 'od zer 'bar ba kha dog sna tshogs pa dag byung ngo //phyag dar dang gyo mo'i phung po de las kyang legs so zhes bya ba'i sgra byung ngo //de bzhin gshegs pa shAkya thub pa legs so legs so//deng ni

4b6(rgyud): ci'i slad du bcom ldan 'das kyis 'khor 'di'i nang du zhu ba 'di skabs dbye bar ci gnang /de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis gnod sbyin gyi sde dpon chenapo lag na rdo rje la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to//lag na rdo rje ring bsrel gyi phung po de

5a1(rgyud): @#//gshegs pa 'bum phrag bye ba til gyi gang bu snyed dgu bcu rtsa dgu'i gtsug tor dang /spyi bo yang de bzhin du gnas so//lag na rdo rje gdan chos kyi rnam grangs 'di gnas pa de ni de bzhin gshegs pa'i

7a6(rgyud): phyogs der yams kyi nad dang /nad 'go ba dang /'khrug pa yang mi 'byung bar 'gyu ra/gnod sbyin dang /srin po dang /'byung po dang /yi dgas dang /'dre dang /brjed byed kyi 'jigs pa yang mi 'byung par 'gyur/gdon thams

7a8(rgyud): tshugs par 'gyur zhing mchod rten de mthong ba tsam gyis thams cad 'byang bar 'gyur ro//phyogs der ba lang gi nad dang /phyugs nad dang /dud 'gro'i skye gnas rnam pa mang po gzhan dag gi nad dang /skyes pa dang 
7b1(rgyud): bud med dang /khye'u dang /bu mo'i nad kyis kyang nam yang tshugs par mi 'gyur ro//dus ma yin par 'chi bas kyang dus ma yin pa'i 'chi bar mi 'gyur/dug gis ma yin/mtshon gyis ma yin zhing me dang 
7b2(rgyud): chus kyang nam yang mi tshugs par 'gyur/pha rol gyi dmag tshogs kyis 'jigs pas ma yin/lo nyes kyi 'jigs pas kyang nam yang mi tshugs bar 'gyur/rgyal po chen po'i dmag gis 'jigs pa yang 
7b3(rgyud): mi 'byung bar 'gyur ro//rgyal po chen po bzhi yang rtag tu rgyun mi 'chad par srung bar 'gyur ro//sa phyogs der srung bar byed/yongs su skyong bar byed do//gnod sbyin gyi sde dpon chen po nyi shu rtsa brgyad po de dag 
7b4(rgyud): kyang rtag tu rgyun mi 'chad bar srung ba dang /skyob pa dang /sbed bar byed par 'gyur ro//rgyu skar nyi shu rtsa brgyad dang /zla ba dang /nyi ma dang /mjug rings chen po yang nyin mtshan 'thun par byed par 'gyur ro//klu'i rgyal po thams 
7b5(rgyud): cad kyang rtag tu rgyun mi 'chad pa mdangs mi 'phrog cing legs pa kho nar char gyi rgyun 'bebs par 'gyur ro//lha thams cad kyang mchod rten chen po de la phyag 'tshal ba dang /bkur sti bya ba dang /mchod pa bya ba'i phyir 
7b6(rgyud): gnas sum bcu rtsa gnyis nas dus gsum du 'ong ngo //yul na gnas pa thams cad kyang mchod rtenadang /gzugs brnyan de'i drung du dus gsum du 'ong ngo /bstod par byed bskor ba byed par 'gyur ro//
7b7(rgyud): lha rnams kyi dbang po brgya byin kyang lha'i bu mo dang /lha'i tshogs rnams dang lhan gcig rtag tu rgyun mi 'chad par mchod rten nam/gzugs brnyan gyi drung du nyin mtshan dus gsum du 'ong ste/phyag 'tshal ba dang /
7b8(rgyud): mchod pa la 'ong ngo //de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyang rtag par dgongs pa dang /byin gyis brlob par 'gyur ro//mchod rten dang /gzugs brnyan ji lta ji lta bu las byed pas 'am/rdo'i 'am/shing gi 'am/
8a1(rgyud): @#//dngul gyi 'am/gser gyi 'am/zangs kyir byed pa dang /chos kyi rnam grangs 'di yang bris te/de'i nang du bzhag na bzhag ma thag tsam gyis rin po che sna bdun gyi rang bzhin du byin gyis brlabs par 'gyur 
8a2(rgyud): ro//'phang dang /them skas dang /kha ran dang /'khor lo'i phreng ba dang /bkra shis dang /gdugs dang /dril bu 'phyang ba dang /ba dan dang /dril bu g.yer ka'i dra ba thams cad kyang rin po che sna bdun gyi rab bzhin du 'gyur ro//
8a3(rgyud): phyogs bzhi kun na yang de bzhin gshegs pa'i gzugs brnyan gnas par 'gyur ro//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi byin gyi rlabs kyis byin gyis brlabs pa'i mchod rten rin po che dang /sku gdung gi snying po'i 
8a4(rgyud): mchod rten dang /mchod gnas su 'gyur ro//sku gzugs dang /mchod rten de 'og min gyi gnas kyi lhas kyang yongs su bskyang ba dang /mchod pas yongs su gzung bar 'gyur ro//de nas bcom ldan 'das la gnod sbyin 
8a5(rgyud): gyi sde dpon chen po lag na rdo rjes 'di skad ces gsol to//bcom ldan 'das chos kyi rnam grangs 'di yon tan gyi khyad par 'di lta bur 'gyur/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/de bzhin gshegs pa 
8a6(rgyud): thams cad kyi byin gyis brlabs kyis snying po gsang ba ring bsrel gyi za ma tog gi phyag rgya'i gzungs yin te/lag na rdo rje des na mthu 'di ni yon tan gyi khyad par 'dilta bur byin gyi rlabs kyis byin kyis rlabs pa yin no//rdo rje 'dzin gyis 
8a7(rgyud): gsol pa/bcom ldan 'das rin po che'i za ma tog gi phyag rgya'i gathungs kyi chos kyi rnam grangs bshad par ci gnang /bcom ldan 'das kyis bkra' stsal pa/lag na rdo rje nyon cig de bzhin gshegs pa 'das 
8a8(rgyud): pa dang /ma byon pa dang /da ltar byung ba dang /gang dag yongs su mya ngan las 'das pa'i sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das thams cad kyi de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku ni gsum ste/chos kyi sku dang /longs spyod rdzogs pa'i 
8b1(rgyud): sku dang /de bzhin du sprul pa'i sku ste/dus gsum pa thams cad kyi ring bsrel gyi za ma tog gi phyag rgya'i gzungs kyi rnam grangs yin no//nam s+t+yaing d+h+wi kA nA na sarba ta thA ga ta nA na/oM b+hu bi b+hu ba na ba tsa To/tsu lu tsu 
8b2(rgyud): lu/d+ha ra d+ha re/sarba ta thA ga tA d+hA tu d+ha re/pad mA ger+b+he/dza ya ba re/a tsa le/sma ra ta thA ga ta d+harma tsakra/bra bar te na badzra/bo d+hi man Da a lang ka ra/a lang kri te/sarba ta thA ga tA/a d+hi Sh+Thi te/bo d+ha ya bo d+ha

8b4(rgyud): sam b+ha ba/sarba ta thA ga ta gu h+ye/d+ha ra Ni mud dre/bud d+he/su bud d+he/sarba ta thA ga ta a d+hi Sh+Thi te d+hA tu gar b+he swA hA/sa ma ya a d+hi Sh+Thi te swA hA/sarba ta thA ga ta hri da ya d+hA tu mud tre swA hA/su tra tiSh+Thi ta stu be ta thA ga ta 
8b5(rgyud): a d+hi Sh+Thi te hU~M hU~M swA hA/oM sarba ta thA ga ta uSh+Ni Sha d+hA tu mudA ni sarba ta thA ga ta d+harma d+hA tu bi b+hu Shi ta a d+hi Sh+Thi te/hu ru hu ru/tu ru tu ru/tu 'um hu 'um swA hA//bcom ldan 'das kyis ring bsrel gyi za ma tog gi phyag

8b8(rgyud): so zhes bya ba byin no//'di ni de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi dam tshig dang /byin gyis brlabs pa yin no//gang na ring bsrel gyi phyag rgya'i gzungs 'di ston par byed dam/mchod rten gyi nang du 'jog 
9a1(rgyud): @#//gam//gzugs brnyan gyi nang du 'jog par byed pa der/de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyang gcig tu rtag tu rgyun mi 'chad par rjes su 'brang zhing bzhugs par 'gyur ro//thams cad du de bzhin

12b3(rgyud): mdzad par 'gyur//ston pa 'gro ba rnams kyi rten//sems can kun gyi don gyi phyir//gang gis bde ba mnga' mdzad des//khyed la bde legasamadzad par 'gyur//skyob pa gang zhig byams pa yi//thugs kyis 'gro ba 'di dag

12b6(rgyud): grub tshogs kyang grub pa des//khyed la bde le gas mdzad par 'gyur//gang zhig skyes pas sa rnams na//nags tshal bcas par rab g.yos shing //sems can thams cad rab dga' des//khyed la bde legs mdzad par 
12b7(rgyud): 'gyur//byang chub snying por gang zhig gis//sa dag rnam drug g.yos gyur cing //bdud yid mi bder gyur pa des//khyed la bde logs mdzad par 'gyur//chos kyi 'khor lo bskor ba na//'phags pa'i bden pa rnams 
12b8(rgyud): gsung ba//thub pa'i snyan pa gang byung des//khyed la bde legasamadzad par 'gyur//yid 'phrog gang gis mu stegs byed//thams cad chos kyis pham mdzad cing //chos kun dbang du mdzad pa des//khyed la ྾྾྾྾྾྾྾྾྾྾྾྾྾྾྾

24b1(rgyud): ces bka' stsal nas/byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po byams pa dang /byang chub sems dpa' de dag dang /dge slong de dag dang /thams cad dang ldan pa'i 'khor de dag dang /lha dang /mi dang /lha ma yin dang /dri zar 
24b2(rgyud): bcas pa'i 'jig rten yid rangs te/bcom ldan 'das kyis gsungs pa la mngon par bstod do////'phags pa sangs rgyas bcu gnyis pa zhes bya ba theg pa chen po'i mdo rdzogs so//rgya gar gyi mkhan 
24b3(rgyud): po dzi na mi tra dang /dA na shI la dang /zhu chen gyi lo ts+tsha ba ban de ye shes sdes bsgyur cing zhus te skad gsar chad kyis kyang bcos nas gtan la phab pa/@#//rgya gar skad du/Ar+ya sap+ta bud 
24b4(rgyud): d+ha ka nA nA ma ma hA yA na sU tra/bod skad du/'phags pa sangs rgyas bdun pa zhes bya ba theg pa chenapo'i mdo//sangs rgyas dang /byang chub sems dba' thams cad la phyag 'tshal lo//'di skad bdag gis thos pa dus 
24b5(rgyud): gcig na/bcom ldan 'das ti se'i ri'i rtse mo lha'i gnas na drang srong gi spyod pa dang ha cang yang mi ring ba zhig na dge slong lnga brgya tsam gyi dge slong gi dge 'dun chen po dang byang chub sems dpa' lnga brgya la 'di lta ste/byang chub 
24b6(rgyud): sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po byams pa dang /byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' che na po nam mkha'i snying po dang /kun tu bzang po dang /mtha' yas me tog dang /kun nas me tog dang /de dag la sogs pa byang chub 
24b7(rgyud): sems dpa' skye ba gcig gis thogs pa sha stag dang thabs gcig tu bzhugs so//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis ri'i rtse mo'i nags khrod de nyid na dge slong zhig 'byung pos zin nas brdol zhing gcer bu gos 
24b8(rgyud): med pa la lag pa bsgreng ste ngu zhing 'dug pa gzigs so//de nas byang chub sems dpa' semasadapa' chen po nam mkha'i snying pos bla gos phrag pa gcig tu gzar te/pus mo g.yas pa'i lha nga sa la btsugs 
25a1(rgyud): nas/bcom ldan 'das ga la ba de logs su thal mo sbyar ba btud de/bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to//bcom ldan 'das nam mkha'i dkyil na kyi hud ces sgra 'byin pa 'di dang /dge slong 
25a2(rgyud): gcer bu gos ma mchis la lag pa stod pa gcig shos 'di yang su zhig lags/de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po nam mkha'i snying po la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to//
25a3(rgyud): nam mkha'i snying po 'di ni nad kyis btab pa yin no//gcig shos 'di yang nad kyis btab pa yin na no//de nas bcom ldan 'das la byang chub sems dpa' sem dpa' chen po nam mkha'i sning po la 'di skad ces

25b4(rgyud): byin na nad thams cad sos par 'gyur ro//las thams cad la yang sbyar bar bya'o//de nas de bzhin gshegs pa gtsug gtor can steng gi nam mkha' la bzhugs te/sems can thams cad la phan pa'i
